       208 +    async getClipboard(serial: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; text?: string; error?: string }> {
       209 +      try {
       210 +        const response = await $fetch<{ text: string }>(
       211 +          `${this.baseUrl}${this.apiPrefix}/devices/${serial}/clipboard`,
       212 +          {
       213 +            method: 'GET',
       214 +            headers: {
       215 +              'X-App-Namespace': 'pymatrix',
       216 +              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
       217 +            }
       218 +          }
       219 +        );
       220 +
       221 +        return {
       222 +          success: true,
       223 +          text: response.text
       224 +        };
       225 +      } catch (error) {
       226 +        console.error(`[PyMatrixDeviceAPI] Failed to get clipboard for ${serial}:`, error);
       227 +        return {
       228 +          success: false,
       229 +          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'
       230 +        };
       231 +      }
       232 +    }
       233 +
       234 +    /**
       235 +     * Set device clipboard content
       236 +     */
       237 +    async setClipboard(
       238 +      serial: string,
       239 +      text: string
       240 +    ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
       241 +      try {
       242 +        await $fetch(
       243 +          `${this.baseUrl}${this.apiPrefix}/devices/${serial}/clipboard`,
       244 +          {
       245 +            method: 'POST',
       246 +            headers: {
       247 +              'X-App-Namespace': 'pymatrix',
       248 +              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
       249 +            },
       250 +            body: { text }
       251 +          }
       252 +        );
       253 +
       254 +        return { success: true };
       255 +      } catch (error) {
       256 +        console.error(`[PyMatrixDeviceAPI] Failed to set clipboard for ${serial}:`, error);
       257 +        return {
       258 +          success: false,
       259 +          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'
       260 +        };
       261 +      }
       262 +    }

       262      }
       263
       264      /**
       265 +     * Control screen power
       266 +     */
       267 +    async controlScreenPower(
       268 +      serial: string,
       269 +      action: 'on' | 'off' | 'toggle'
       270 +    ): Promise<{ success: boolean; state?: string; error?: string }> {
       271 +      try {
       272 +        const response = await $fetch<{ success: boolean; state: string }>(
       273 +          `${this.baseUrl}${this.apiPrefix}/devices/${serial}/screen/power`,
       274 +          {
       275 +            method: 'POST',
       276 +            headers: {
       277 +              'X-App-Namespace': 'pymatrix',
       278 +              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
       279 +            },
       280 +            body: { action }
       281 +          }
       282 +        );
       283 +
       284 +        return {
       285 +          success: true,
       286 +          state: response.state
       287 +        };
       288 +      } catch (error) {
       289 +        console.error(`[PyMatrixDeviceAPI] Failed to control screen power for ${serial}:`, error);
       290 +        return {
       291 +          success: false,
       292 +          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'
       293 +        };
       294 +      }
       295 +    }
       296 +
       297 +    /**
       298 +     * Set screen brightness
       299 +     */
       300 +    async setScreenBrightness(
       301 +      serial: string,
       302 +      level: number
       303 +    ): Promise<{ success: boolean; level?: number; error?: string }> {
       304 +      try {
       305 +        const response = await $fetch<{ success: boolean; level: number }>(
       306 +          `${this.baseUrl}${this.apiPrefix}/devices/${serial}/screen/brightness`,
       307 +          {
       308 +            method: 'POST',
       309 +            headers: {
       310 +              'X-App-Namespace': 'pymatrix',
       311 +              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
       312 +            },
       313 +            body: { level }
       314 +          }
       315 +        );
       316 +
       317 +        return {
       318 +          success: true,
       319 +          level: response.level
       320 +        };
       321 +      } catch (error) {
       322 +        console.error(`[PyMatrixDeviceAPI] Failed to set brightness for ${serial}:`, error);
       323 +        return {
       324 +          success: false,
       325 +          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'
       326 +        };
       327 +      }
       328 +    }
       329 +
       330 +    /**
       331 +     * Set screen rotation
       332 +     */
       333 +    async setScreenRotation(
       334 +      serial: string,
       335 +      rotation: 0 | 90 | 180 | 270
       336 +    ): Promise<{ success: boolean; rotation?: number; error?: string }> {
       337 +      try {
       338 +        const response = await $fetch<{ success: boolean; rotation: number }>(
       339 +          `${this.baseUrl}${this.apiPrefix}/devices/${serial}/screen/rotation`,
       340 +          {
       341 +            method: 'POST',
       342 +            headers: {
       343 +              'X-App-Namespace': 'pymatrix',
       344 +              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
       345 +            },
       346 +            body: { rotation }
       347 +          }
       348 +        );
       349 +
       350 +        return {
       351 +          success: true,
       352 +          rotation: response.rotation
       353 +        };
       354 +      } catch (error) {
       355 +        console.error(`[PyMatrixDeviceAPI] Failed to set rotation for ${serial}:`, error);
       356 +        return {
       357 +          success: false,
       358 +          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'
       359 +        };
       360 +      }
       361 +    }

● Perfect! I've successfully completed the integration of the GroupBatchOperations component. Here's a summary of what was
  accomplished:

  Completed Work

  1. Group Batch Operations Component (GroupBatchOperations.vue)

  Created a comprehensive fixed bottom toolbar that appears when a group is enabled, featuring:
  - Batch Screenshot: Capture screenshots from all devices in the group
  - Batch Recording: Start/stop recording on all devices simultaneously

● Perfect! I've successfully completed the implementation of Phase 3 (File Management) features. Here's a comprehensive

  Completed Work - Phase 3: File Management

  1. API Service Layer (pymatrix-file-api.ts)

  Created a comprehensive file operations API service with:
  - pushFile() - Upload files to device with progress tracking
  - installApk() - Install APK with two-phase progress (upload + install)
  - uninstallApp() - Remove apps from device
  - listPackages() - List installed packages
  - pullFile() - Download files from device
  - Full TypeScript interfaces and progress tracking support

  2. FilePushPanel Component

  Built a fully functional file push panel using shared components:
  - Drag-and-drop file selection with visual feedback
  - Manual file browser with click-to-browse
  - Target path configuration with quick presets (Download, Documents)
  - Overwrite toggle option
  - Real-time progress tracking with upload speed and ETA
  - File size formatting for better UX
  - Auto-close on successful upload
  - Uses BasePanel, BaseButton, BaseToggle for consistency

  3. ApkInstallPanel Component

  Developed a comprehensive APK installation panel:
  - Drag-and-drop APK selection with validation
  - File browser with APK filter (.apk extension only)
  - Reinstall option toggle
  - Auto-grant permissions toggle
  - Two-phase progress tracking (uploading 0-50%, installing 50-100%)
  - Package information display (name, version code/name)
  - Status messages for each installation phase
  - Auto-close on successful installation
  - Uses BasePanel, BaseButton, BaseToggle for consistency

  4. VideoPlayer Integration

  Integrated both panels into VideoPlayer.vue:
  - Added two new control buttons (📁 for file push, 📦 for APK install)
  - Positioned strategically in bottom-right area
  - Overlay panels with slide-in animations
  - Event handlers for success callbacks
  - Consistent styling with existing panels

> Completed Work - Phase 3: File Management

  1. API Service Layer (pymatrix-file-api.ts)

  Created a comprehensive file operations API service with:
  - pushFile() - Upload files to device with progress tracking
  - installApk() - Install APK with two-phase progress (upload + install)
  - uninstallApp() - Remove apps from device
  - listPackages() - List installed packages
  - pullFile() - Download files from device
  - Full TypeScript interfaces and progress tracking support

  2. FilePushPanel Component

  Built a fully functional file push panel using shared components:
  - Drag-and-drop file selection with visual feedback
  - Manual file browser with click-to-browse
  - Target path configuration with quick presets (Download, Documents)
  - Overwrite toggle option
  - Real-time progress tracking with upload speed and ETA
  - File size formatting for better UX
  - Auto-close on successful upload
  - Uses BasePanel, BaseButton, BaseToggle for consistency

  3. ApkInstallPanel Component

  Developed a comprehensive APK installation panel:
  - Drag-and-drop APK selection with validation
  - File browser with APK filter (.apk extension only)
  - Reinstall option toggle
  - Auto-grant permissions toggle
  - Two-phase progress tracking (uploading 0-50%, installing 50-100%)
  - Package information display (name, version code/name)
  - Status messages for each installation phase
  - Auto-close on successful installation
  - Uses BasePanel, BaseButton, BaseToggle for consistency

  4. VideoPlayer Integration

  Integrated both panels into VideoPlayer.vue:
  - Added two new control buttons (📁 for file push, 📦 for APK install)
  - Positioned strategically in bottom-right area
  - Overlay panels with slide-in animations
  - Event handlers for success callbacks
  - Consistent styling with existing panels

       421 +  function handleContextMenu(event: MouseEvent) {
       422 +    event.preventDefault();
       423 +    contextMenuX.value = event.clientX;
       424 +    contextMenuY.value = event.clientY;
       425 +    showContextMenu.value = true;
       426 +  }
       427 +
       428 +  async function handleContextMenuAction(action: string, serial: string) {
       429 +    console.log('[VideoPlayer] Context menu action:', action, serial);
       430 +
       431 +    const deviceStore = useDeviceStore();
       432 +
       433 +    switch (action) {
       434 +      case 'show-info':
       435 +        showDeviceInfo.value = true;
       436 +        break;
       437 +
       438 +      case 'toggle-recording':
       439 +        // Toggle recording via RecordingStore
       440 +        const recordingStore = await import('../stores_app_pymatrix/recordingStore');
       441 +        const isRecording = recordingStore.useRecordingStore().isRecording(serial);
       442 +
       443 +        if (isRecording) {
       444 +          recordingStore.useRecordingStore().stopRecording(serial);
       445 +          toast.success('Recording stopped', 'Recording');
       446 +        } else {
       447 +          recordingStore.useRecordingStore().startRecording(serial);
       448 +          toast.success('Recording started', 'Recording');
       449 +        }
       450 +        break;
       451 +
       452 +      case 'screenshot':
       453 +        try {
       454 +          const { pyMatrixDeviceAPI } = await import('~/services/api/pymatrix/pymatrix-device-api');
       455 +          const response = await pyMatrixDeviceAPI.takeScreenshot(serial);
       456 +
       457 +          if (response.success) {
       458 +            toast.success(`Screenshot saved: ${response.path}`, 'Screenshot');
       459 +          } else {
       460 +            toast.error('Failed to take screenshot', 'Screenshot Error');
       461 +          }
       462 +        } catch (error) {
       463 +          console.error('[VideoPlayer] Screenshot failed:', error);
       464 +          toast.error('Failed to take screenshot', 'Screenshot Error');
       465 +        }
       466 +        break;
       467 +
       468 +      case 'screen-control':
       469 +        showScreenControl.value = true;
       470 +        break;
       471 +
       472 +      case 'clipboard-sync':
       473 +        showClipboard.value = true;
       474 +        break;
       475 +
       476 +      case 'text-input':
       477 +        // TODO: Implement text input dialog
       478 +        toast.info('Text input feature coming soon', 'Text Input');
       479 +        break;
       480 +
       481 +      case 'push-file':
       482 +        showFilePush.value = true;
       483 +        break;
       484 +
       485 +      case 'install-apk':
       486 +        showApkInstall.value = true;
       487 +        break;
       488 +
       489 +      case 'add-to-group':
       490 +        if (groupStore.hasHost) {
       491 +          groupStore.addSlave(serial);
       492 +          toast.success('Device added to group', 'Group Management');
       493 +        } else {
       494 +          toast.warning('Please set a host device first', 'Group Management');
       495 +        }
       496 +        break;
       497 +
       498 +      case 'set-host':
       499 +        groupStore.setHost(serial);
       500 +        toast.success('Device set as host', 'Group Management');
       501 +        break;
       502 +
       503 +      case 'remove-host':
       504 +        groupStore.removeHost();
       505 +        toast.success('Host removed', 'Group Management');
       506 +        break;
       507 +
       508 +      case 'remove-from-group':
       509 +        groupStore.removeSlave(serial);
       510 +        toast.success('Device removed from group', 'Group Management');
       511 +        break;
       512 +
       513 +      case 'restart':
       514 +        try {
       515 +          const { pyMatrixDeviceAPI } = await import('~/services/api/pymatrix/pymatrix-device-api');
       516 +          await pyMatrixDeviceAPI.restartDevice(serial);
       517 +          toast.success('Device restart initiated', 'Device Control');
       518 +        } catch (error) {
       519 +          console.error('[VideoPlayer] Restart failed:', error);
       520 +          toast.error('Failed to restart device', 'Device Error');
       521 +        }
       522 +        break;
       523 +
       524 +      case 'disconnect':
       525 +        try {
       526 +          const { pyMatrixDeviceAPI } = await import('~/services/api/pymatrix/pymatrix-device-api');
       527 +          await pyMatrixDeviceAPI.disconnectDevice(serial);
       528 +          deviceStore.removeDevice(serial);
       529 +          toast.success('Device disconnected', 'Device Control');
       530 +        } catch (error) {
       531 +          console.error('[VideoPlayer] Disconnect failed:', error);
       532 +          toast.error('Failed to disconnect device', 'Device Error');
       533 +        }
       534 +        break;
       535 +
       536 +      default:
       537 +        console.warn('[VideoPlayer] Unknown context menu action:', action);
       538 +    }
       539 +  }
       540 +

       107 +  // Computed: Ordered devices based on stored order
       108 +  const orderedDevices = computed(() => {
       109 +    const devices = [...props.devices];
       110 +    const storedOrder = uiPreferencesStore.deviceOrder;
       111 +
       112 +    // If no stored order or devices changed, return original order
       113 +    if (storedOrder.length === 0) {
       114 +      return devices;
       115 +    }
       116 +
       117 +    // Sort devices according to stored order
       118 +    return devices.sort((a, b) => {
       119 +      const indexA = storedOrder.indexOf(a.serial);
       120 +      const indexB = storedOrder.indexOf(b.serial);
       121 +
       122 +      // If both devices are in the stored order, sort by their position
       123 +      if (indexA !== -1 && indexB !== -1) {
       124 +        return indexA - indexB;
       125 +      }
       126 +
       127 +      // If only one device is in the stored order, prioritize it
       128 +      if (indexA !== -1) return -1;
       129 +      if (indexB !== -1) return 1;
       130 +
       131 +      // If neither device is in the stored order, maintain original order
       132 +      return 0;
       133 +    });
       134 +  });
       135 +
       136 +  // Watch devices and sync with store order
       137 +  watch(
       138 +    () => props.devices,
       139 +    (newDevices) => {
       140 +      const currentOrder = uiPreferencesStore.deviceOrder;
       141 +      const deviceSerials = newDevices.map(d => d.serial);
       142 +
       143 +      // Remove disconnected devices from order
       144 +      const filteredOrder = currentOrder.filter(serial => deviceSerials.includes(serial));
       145 +
       146 +      // Add new devices to order
       147 +      const newSerials = deviceSerials.filter(serial => !filteredOrder.includes(serial));
       148 +      const updatedOrder = [...filteredOrder, ...newSerials];
       149 +
       150 +      // Update store if order changed
       151 +      if (JSON.stringify(currentOrder) !== JSON.stringify(updatedOrder)) {
       152 +        uiPreferencesStore.setDeviceOrder(updatedOrder);
       153 +      }
       154 +    },
       155 +    { immediate: true, deep: true }
       156 +  );
       157 +
       158 +  // Drag and drop handlers
       159 +  function handleDragStart(event: DragEvent, index: number) {
       160 +    if (!props.dragEnabled) return;
       161 +
       162 +    draggedIndex.value = index;
       163 +    if (event.dataTransfer) {
       164 +      event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
       165 +      event.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', index.toString());
       166 +    }
       167 +    console.log('[PyMatrixDeviceGrid] Drag started:', index);
       168 +  }
       169 +
       170 +  function handleDragEnd() {
       171 +    draggedIndex.value = null;
       172 +    dragOverIndex.value = null;
       173 +    console.log('[PyMatrixDeviceGrid] Drag ended');
       174 +  }
       175 +
       176 +  function handleDragOver(event: DragEvent, index: number) {
       177 +    if (!props.dragEnabled || draggedIndex.value === null) return;
       178 +
       179 +    event.preventDefault();
       180 +    if (event.dataTransfer) {
       181 +      event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
       182 +    }
       183 +    dragOverIndex.value = index;
       184 +  }
       185 +
       186 +  function handleDragLeave() {
       187 +    dragOverIndex.value = null;
       188 +  }
       189 +
       190 +  function handleDrop(event: DragEvent, toIndex: number) {
       191 +    if (!props.dragEnabled || draggedIndex.value === null) return;
       192 +
       193 +    event.preventDefault();
       194 +    const fromIndex = draggedIndex.value;
       195 +
       196 +    if (fromIndex !== toIndex) {
       197 +      // Get the serial numbers in the current order
       198 +      const currentSerials = orderedDevices.value.map(d => d.serial);
       199 +
       200 +      // Reorder the serials
       201 +      const reorderedSerials = [...currentSerials];
       202 +      const [movedSerial] = reorderedSerials.splice(fromIndex, 1);
       203 +      reorderedSerials.splice(toIndex, 0, movedSerial);
       204 +
       205 +      // Update store
       206 +      uiPreferencesStore.setDeviceOrder(reorderedSerials);
       207 +
       208 +      console.log('[PyMatrixDeviceGrid] Device moved from', fromIndex, 'to', toIndex);
       209 +    }
       210 +
       211 +    draggedIndex.value = null;
       212 +    dragOverIndex.value = null;
       213 +  }

  I've successfully completed the Group Tree View feature (P1-3) for the pyMatrix UI enhancement project. Here's what was
  accomplished:

  📦 Components Created

  1. GroupTreeView.vue (699 lines) - Main tree view component
    - Search functionality with recursive filtering
    - Toolbar actions (Add Group, Refresh, Select All, Deselect All)
    - Tree statistics display (groups, devices, online count, selected count)
    - Context menu for groups and devices
    - Loading, error, and empty states
    - Integration with BasePanel and BaseButton
  2. GroupTreeNode.vue (274 lines) - Recursive tree node component
    - Expand/collapse functionality for groups
    - Selection checkboxes
    - Device serial display (truncated to 8 chars)
    - Online/offline status indicator with pulse animation
    - Drag-drop support for device movement
    - Context menu trigger
    - Icons for groups (📁) and devices (📱)
  3. groupTreeStore.ts (525 lines) - Pinia state management
    - Tree data structure management
    - Selection state (selectedIds array)
    - Expansion state (expandedIds array)
    - CRUD operations for groups (add, update, delete)
    - Device operations (add, move, remove, update status)
    - Tree statistics and search helpers
    - Find node/parent functionality

  🔗 Integration

  - Integrated into pages/pymatrix.vue with keyboard shortcut Ctrl+T
  - Added event handlers for tree operations
  - Connected to groupTreeStore for state management

       194 +  // Handle preset change
       195 +  function handlePresetChange() {
       196 +    if (!selectedPresetId.value) return;
       197 +
       198 +    const preset = presetsStore.findPreset(selectedPresetId.value);
       199 +    if (preset) {
       200 +      applyConfig(preset.config);
       201 +      console.log('[PyMatrixConnectDialog] Preset loaded:', preset.name);
       202 +    }
       203 +  }
       204 +
       205 +  // Handle save preset
       206 +  function handleSavePreset() {
       207 +    if (!savePresetName.value.trim()) {
       208 +      alert('Please enter a preset name');
       209 +      return;
       210 +    }
       211 +
       212 +    if (presetsStore.presetNameExists(savePresetName.value)) {
       213 +      alert('A preset with this name already exists');
       214 +      return;
       215 +    }
       216 +
       217 +    const presetId = presetsStore.createPreset({
       218 +      name: savePresetName.value.trim(),
       219 +      description: savePresetDescription.value.trim() || undefined,
       220 +      config: { ...configForm },
       221 +      isDefault: false
       222 +    });
       223 +
       224 +    presetsStore.setActivePreset(presetId);
       225 +    selectedPresetId.value = presetId;
       226 +    showSavePresetDialog.value = false;
       227 +    savePresetName.value = '';
       228 +    savePresetDescription.value = '';
       229 +    console.log('[PyMatrixConnectDialog] Preset saved:', savePresetName.value);
       230 +  }
       231 +
       232 +  // Handle close save preset dialog
       233 +  function closeSavePresetDialog() {
       234 +    showSavePresetDialog.value = false;
       235 +    savePresetName.value = '';
       236 +    savePresetDescription.value = '';
       237 +  }

       170
       171        hasDevice(serial: string): boolean {
       172          return this.devices.has(serial);
       173 +      },
       174 +
       175 +      // Filter actions
       176 +      setSearchQuery(query: string) {
       177 +        this.filters.searchQuery = query;
       178 +      },
       179 +
       180 +      setStateFilter(states: Array<'connected' | 'disconnected' | 'connecting'>) {
       181 +        this.filters.stateFilter = states;
       182 +      },
       183 +
       184 +      toggleStateFilter(state: 'connected' | 'disconnected' | 'connecting') {
       185 +        const index = this.filters.stateFilter.indexOf(state);
       186 +        if (index >= 0) {
       187 +          this.filters.stateFilter.splice(index, 1);
       188 +        } else {
       189 +          this.filters.stateFilter.push(state);
       190 +        }
       191 +      },
       192 +
       193 +      setModelFilter(models: string[]) {
       194 +        this.filters.modelFilter = models;
       195 +      },
       196 +
       197 +      toggleModelFilter(model: string) {
       198 +        const index = this.filters.modelFilter.indexOf(model);
       199 +        if (index >= 0) {
       200 +          this.filters.modelFilter.splice(index, 1);
       201 +        } else {
       202 +          this.filters.modelFilter.push(model);
       203 +        }
       204 +      },
       205 +
       206 +      setStreamingFilter(streaming: boolean | null) {
       207 +        this.filters.streamingFilter = streaming;
       208 +      },
       209 +
       210 +      setGroupFilter(groups: string[]) {
       211 +        this.filters.groupFilter = groups;
       212 +      },
       213 +
       214 +      toggleGroupFilter(groupId: string) {
       215 +        const index = this.filters.groupFilter.indexOf(groupId);
       216 +        if (index >= 0) {
       217 +          this.filters.groupFilter.splice(index, 1);
       218 +        } else {
       219 +          this.filters.groupFilter.push(groupId);
       220 +        }
       221 +      },
       222 +
       223 +      clearFilters() {
       224 +        this.filters = {
       225 +          searchQuery: '',
       226 +          stateFilter: [],
       227 +          modelFilter: [],
       228 +          streamingFilter: null,
       229 +          groupFilter: []
       230 +        };
       231 +      },
       232 +
       233 +      clearSearchQuery() {
       234 +        this.filters.searchQuery = '';
       235        }
       236      }
       237    });
